Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the bio object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 306. The moduli space has dimension at most 426 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on bio structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 306. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 426. QED.

Statement: The bio operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 574. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 469 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

For the inductive step, we decompose the bio structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 469. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
